A current SLE case retained 19/20 and the other inspected NASDAQ cases passed. The 11 NSE cases were not similarity failures; their sealed whole-file fingerprints were invalidated by append/update drift and are classified separately as blocked inputs.

Attribution: TVTX lost HLIT/CDMO after local admission when equal RRF placed them at 28,175/26,064. CAKE lost FIBK at local rank 28 and four more through global ranks 23,708–102,220. Raising the local cap to 40 created 909,516 intermediate rows and still did not provide a guarantee. The difficult CAKE exact total has only 0.017 Spearman correlation with the current weighted proxy across its exact top-20.

Why earlier gates did not close this: The original plans correctly left complete-universe recall open, built exact authorities and required a later per-case comparison. However, sampled-real gates used only 2,658 NASDAQ candidates and synthetic tests did not model millions of distractors, local-rank-28 positives or cross-view decoy swarms. The process omitted a pre-build candidate-to-authority alignment POC and allowed the expensive 24-authority build before testing production proxy geometry on a few exhaustive cases.

Control correction: The old registry is development evidence after inspection. Every candidate route must now map to exact fields/weights and emit stage-level loss attribution. A new untouched registry is required after the repair; an old failed holdout cannot become independent validation again.

L-045 — M04R second-pass research, bounded POCs and frozen remediation architecture

Research: Three independent audits reviewed the local plans/implementation plus exact/progressive time-series retrieval, elastic-distance bounds, multivariate/variable-length methods, motif/structural finance work, vector indexes, storage formats and current open-source systems. The decision record covers GEMINI, UCR/LB_Keogh/LB_Improved/GLB/EAPrunedDTW, PAA/Haar/SFA, MESSI/Hercules/Coconut/Dumpy, ULISSE/CIVET/VALMOD, Matrix Profile, PIP/directional change, Faiss/USearch/RaBitQ/SVS, packed/mmap/compressed stores and evaluation leakage. No repository is exact for the current pair-scaled, path-normalized composite.

Proposal POC: Removing the local cap and globally applying exact component weights did not solve retrieval: TVTX remained 18/20 at pool 20,000 and CAKE improved only to 18/20, with MSCC/DSGX at ranks 95,033/120,819. Robust/percentile normalizations and a fixed route union were worse. In contrast, the existing query-specific native lower-bound frontier placed every exact top-20 within rank 56 across TVTX, CAKE and SLE.

Bound POCs: A 90-value compact bound was safe on 22,808 real and 100,000 random/adversarial pairs but retained only 14.90% of native pruning. Pair-scaled PAA/moment layouts retained 12.37–13.64%; DCT/SFA with deployable denominators retained 20.1–23.6%. The selected full float16 error-corrected lower-bound row produced zero violations on 23,009 real windows and 50,000 pairs per denominator variant, pruned 99.19% versus native 99.36%, and retained 99.83% of native pruning. It projects to 8.43 GiB for 3.82m NASDAQ rows before IDs/masks/metadata.

Kernel POCs: Warm compact arithmetic reached 2.03m rows/second at 249 MB, showing arithmetic is not the compact bottleneck. The Python full quantile-aware bound processed about 9,084 rows/second, projecting to roughly seven minutes before I/O/refinement and requiring optimization. A compiled two-row DTW kernel exactly matched 2,000 Python references and had zero unsafe abandons/mismatches on 100,000 threshold cases; early abandon triggered only 429 times, so it is a survivor optimization rather than the universe solution.

Failed harness attempts retained: The compact random fixture initially tried to broadcast three scalars into three 128-value rows; the frontier-rank POC initially joined a manifest-relative query directory twice. Both failed before emitting evidence or changing production artifacts and were corrected in the experimental harnesses.

Evidence digests: proposal a9f361cf…d7c95; native-frontier ranks 8622e20c…75582; compact bound fc6267cf…ed23c; pair-scaled bound e1537e62…47dc; spectral bound 0af27675…ea82; full quantized bound d153d47e…92b0; compact scan 701e8262…a106; DTW kernel 7f49ab08…a7671.

Decision: Freeze a deterministic filter–refine architecture with an optional proposal τ seeder, a packed full float16/error-radius distance-v1 bound store, blockwise complete accounting, compiled exact survivor completion, separate progressive/certified modes and batched multi-query scans. A full store is blocked until a 1% packed performance gate passes and free space satisfies a 30 GiB shadow-build precondition. Distance-v2 remains a separately versioned later research branch.

Documents: docs/m04r-research-and-poc-report.html contains the research/POC evidence and explicit rejected alternatives. docs/m04r-certified-nasdaq-retrieval-plan.html contains the final numbered, test-gated implementation sequence. Experimental code lives under experiments/m04r; generated 120-KiB JSON evidence remains under the ignored artifact root.

L-046 — M04R-00 deterministic incident attribution

What: Added a dedicated diagnose-m04r-incident command and readable/machine reports. For every frozen authority neighbour it records authority-certified eligibility, exact total and component distances, persisted-store eligibility, all per-symbol view ranks, local-cap admission, global view/fusion ranks, final pool rank and one explicit terminal reason. It reconstructs the production local-cap plus reciprocal-rank-fusion route and checks every resulting authority rank against the preserved M04-v1 case artifacts.

Complete accounting: All 23 locked holdouts and all 460 exact top-20 neighbours are represented. The 12 unchanged NASDAQ cases were fully analyzed with zero candidate-rank parity errors. All 11 NSE holdouts are labelled blocked_input_version because their frozen stock and/or benchmark file fingerprints differ after data updates; none is counted as a similarity failure. Current SLE has 19 included + 1 local-cap loss, while still meeting the old 95% inclusion threshold.

Frozen failure facts: Historical TVTX has 18 included + 2 fusion-pool losses. Historical CAKE has 15 included + 1 local-cap loss + 4 fusion-pool losses. These counts are executable acceptance conditions, not narrative interpretation. Any missing target, unexpected eligibility state, manifest drift, preserved-rank mismatch or incomplete case accounting fails the diagnostic.

Repeatability and leakage: Three complete full-universe executions—including a final pass after adding stricter identity, cardinality and impossible-state guards—produced baseline digest 5adcbd69…6a81f1 and byte-identical machine SHA-256 aab32b24…24d64. The input contract permits only frozen authorities/registries, preserved M04 cases, raw query and benchmark OHLCV, and immutable view shards. The report records zero opened outcome inputs and real_forward_outcomes_accessed=false.

Verification: Focused tests independently rebuild a view store and require target-trace pool ranks to equal the production search route exactly; direct tests separate local, fusion, eligibility and missing-store reasons; artifact tests require deterministic bytes and digest binding. The complete repository suite passes 165/165, Python compilation succeeds, edited/generated HTML parses, and the source diff has no whitespace errors.

Artifacts: Generated readable report config/data/analogues/m04r-incident/m04r-incident.html; 708-KiB machine report m04r-incident.json; immutable gate history under gates/history/m04r_incident_attribution. Generated evidence remains under the ignored artifact root; the implementation, tests, command and this log are versioned.

Boundary and next: This checkpoint proves why the old candidate route lost known exact neighbours; it does not repair retrieval or validate trading outcomes. Proceed to M04R-01 canonical causal-prefix digests so append-only daily updates stop invalidating historical queries while historical revisions remain detectable.

L-047 — M04R-01 canonical causal-prefix provenance

What: Added canonical-ohlcv-prefix-v1, a storage-format-independent SHA-256 over ordered timestamp/open/high/low/close/volume values visible through an explicit cutoff. Numeric inputs are normalized to float64, timestamps to nanoseconds, negative zero and missing values are canonicalized, and physical file SHA-256 remains available separately for byte-level audit. Stock and benchmark sources expose the same prefix API, and a versioned generation digest binds dataset, query cutoff, representation version and all supplied prefixes.

Why: The old whole-file fingerprint treated a harmless daily append exactly like a historical correction. The new identity leaves an older query unchanged after future appends or equivalent Parquet rewrites, while any insertion, deletion or value revision at/before the cutoff changes the digest. Duplicate timestamps and raw source reordering are rejected rather than silently sorted into apparent validity. A query at a newly appended cutoff receives a different generation digest.

Synthetic verification: Tests cover equivalent rewrites with a different Parquet compression/dtype and changed physical SHA; future append invariance; historical value revision, row deletion and insertion; duplicate and reordered timestamps; preservation of raw-order warnings through the source adapter; identical benchmark rules; and new-cutoff generation change. All six new causal-prefix tests pass.

Real NASDAQ verification: The verify-m04r-causal-prefixes gate validated all 12 frozen NASDAQ authorities. It executed 96 stock/benchmark physical-binding, repeatability, future-mutation and historical-revision checks with zero failures, zero false append invalidations and zero accepted historical revisions. Two executions produced result digest aeaa1c37…1732dd and byte-identical machine SHA-256 979c069f…a3d3d. No outcome input was accessed.

Non-retroactivity: The 11 legacy NSE holdouts remain blocked_input_version. Because their v1 registry stored only whole-file hashes, a causal prefix existing before the append cannot be reconstructed as prior evidence without assuming the history was unchanged. New registries and immutable packed-store generations can bind this prefix contract; old Gate 12 artifacts remain unchanged.

Verification and artifacts: The complete repository suite passes 171/171. Generated reports are config/data/analogues/m04r-causal-prefixes/m04r-causal-prefixes.html and m04r-causal-prefixes.json, with immutable gate history under gates/history/m04r_causal_prefixes. Generated evidence remains ignored; implementation, tests, CLI and this log are versioned.

Next: M04R-02 must freeze the exact distance-v1 mathematics and implement an independent slow reference before any lower-bound proof is promoted.

L-048 — M04R-02 frozen distance-v1 and independent audit

What: Frozen contract market-analogue-distance-v1-contract records all seven weights, channel groups, missingness, pair scaling, samples, price blend, dependent multivariate DTW channels, local RMS cost, Sakoe–Chiba band, stable predecessor order, realized-path normalization, structural comparison, numerical precision, eligibility, sorting and diversity rules. Its digest is 458e42807d5b69faf5616b4a037c4c0f52003344f9678e14b9549eaa878575a6. Forward outcomes and setup labels are excluded.

Independent implementation: Added a deliberately slow scorer that does not import production distance code. It independently implements finite-value sorting, linear percentiles, population standard deviation, pair scaling, rigid groups, dynamic programming, exact tie-path reconstruction and weighted composition. Tests cover every synthetic family, missing context, constant and extreme finite inputs, unequal paths, tied rounded inputs, identity, symmetry, unit invariance, sliding-batch representations and future benchmark mutation.

Defect found and fixed: The independent scorer exposed that stored float32 coarse vectors were subtracted and standardized before promotion. Ordinary differences were around 2e-9, while an extreme finite fixture showed a material discrepancy. Scalar production and the vectorized exact-batch comparator now promote coarse arrays to float64 before pair statistics and subtraction. The change is intentional numerical correction, not a metric redesign; refreshed values remain within the declared 1e-6 practical tolerance of all preserved authorities.

Bound proof and falsification gate: The contract gives the algebraic proof for the existing native symmetric multivariate LB_Keogh under distance-v1's pair-scaled matrices and path-length normalization: every candidate position is visited, envelope deviation lower-bounds every legal local cost, summed minima lower-bound path cost, and division by the maximum possible path length cannot exceed exact realized-path normalization. A compiled harness compared 2,000 cases with production, exhaustively checked all 729 pairs of length-three ternary series, then tested 1,000,000 pair-scaled constant, random-walk, spike, affine, reversed, equal/unequal-length and 64×4-channel pairs. It found zero unsafe values at absolute tolerance 1e-12; maximum bound/exact ratio was 0.71059. Deterministic evidence digest is 6d94f003…371af. This evidence supports the proof and does not certify the separately deferred float16/error-radius bound.

Failed harness checks retained: The first two million-pair executions correctly returned nonzero after the production-reference cross-check applied the bound's absolute 1e-12 threshold to adversarial costs in the millions. Bound safety itself had zero violations. The harness now keeps absolute 1e-12 for LB ≤ exact, but uses a separate scale-aware 1e-9 relative tolerance only for compiled-versus-Python kernel equivalence; observed maxima were 5.42e-10 exact and 3.92e-10 bound.

Real NASDAQ evidence: The outcome-blind verifier rebuilt every query and stored top-20 neighbour for all 12 authorities, totaling 240 pairs. Maximum production-versus-independent total/component deltas are 4.44e-16/8.88e-16; maximum refreshed-versus-stored authority deltas are 1.54e-8/1.92e-7. Alignment, identity, symmetry and finite-output failures are all zero; triangle inequality is diagnostic-only and had zero sampled violations. Two complete executions produced identical result digest 61a105f0…3be77, machine SHA-256 6bb0aabc…66e86 and contract-file SHA-256 b06008c8…900a8.

Verification and artifacts: Focused distance/batch/CLI tests pass 23/23; the complete suite passes 180/180. Generated readable and machine evidence is under config/data/analogues/m04r-distance-v1; the million-pair record is config/data/analogues/poc/m04r/distance-v1-bound-gate.json. Generated evidence remains ignored; implementation, contract, verifier, tests, experiment and this log are versioned.

Boundary and next: M04R-02 proves and audits native distance-v1; it does not repair candidate retrieval, certify the future quantized bound or validate outcomes. M04R-03 must remove duplicated scalar/sliding feature logic behind one exact-aligned causal kernel before M04R-04 hardens the packed bound.

L-049 — M04R-03 shared exact-aligned feature kernel

What: Scalar represent and sliding_exact_representations now converge on one materialize_exact_representations function for coarse, optional 48/64-sample fields and masks, stages and directional structure. Scalar calls the same vectorized channel-row implementation for the exact fields while retaining its dense channel DataFrame for readable reports and the separately versioned multiresolution state.

Exact-aligned proposal source: Added contract exact-aligned-feature-kernel-v1, digest 3769b076a4fb234c71407638fdaaa0f8d325d8525182d6cba5d58be636c0a8ea. Its lossless 1,376-value float64 row contains 128 coarse values, all nineteen 48-sample channels, all four 64-sample DTW channels, 48 stage values, nine structural values and 23 explicit presence bits. Encode/decode preserves every distance-v1 field and mask exactly. This is the common source for M04R-05's compact-layout experiments; it is intentionally neither a compact production row nor a certified lower bound.

Legacy boundary: The existing 240-value view-signature-v1 remains historical proxy evidence because its range/body/volume/shock/stage/drawdown meanings differ from frozen exact fields. M04R-03 does not rename it, reinterpret old stores or claim it is exact-aligned. New code records legacy_view_signature_equivalent=false and legacy_proxy_used_as_exact_feature=false. Synthetic and determinism gates: Tests cover every morphology family, every field and mask, missing benchmark, constant prices, zero volume, price/volume unit changes, post-cutoff stock and benchmark mutation, whole/chunked equality, serial/four-thread digest identity, finite output and exact feature round-trip. Lossless planted clones rank first for every family, while invariant positives beat both reversed-return and context-flipped critical negatives with zero inversion.

Performance: The current NASDAQ registry reran 600 windows across six symbols. Minimum speedup is 31.1365×, aggregate speedup 31.6848×, peak RSS 353.64 MB, maximum total delta 8.88e-16 and component delta 1.78e-15. The combined NSE+NASDAQ command stopped immediately because the previously documented NSE benchmark fingerprint is stale; it was not bypassed or reported as a kernel result.

Boundary and next: Exact feature semantics are consolidated, but candidate completeness is still not repaired and old view stores are not promoted. M04R-04 must now independently prove the full float16/error-radius bound against this frozen row before any packed build.

L-050 — M04R-04 hardened full float16/error-radius bound

What: Promoted the selected POC into contract distance-v1-full-f16-bound-v1, digest 692e5f40b79397245d65604268c314790347fd0db124ddf985e8367ad36690d1. A query-independent row stores every non-DTW distance-v1 field as float16, exact presence masks and 41 candidate-specific RMS/max quantization radii rounded outward to float32. Values that are non-finite or outside float16 fail closed; they are never clipped, saturated or assigned a bound. Scalar and vectorized batch scorers are implemented, together with a separately written slow reference using independent percentile, standard-deviation and RMS code.

Proof: Reverse triangle inequality lowers each quantized numerator by its stored RMS error. Standard deviation is the norm of a centered vector, so candidate-half error changes the joined deviation by at most error_rms/sqrt(2). Linear quantiles are L-infinity Lipschitz, so adding twice the maximum error upper-bounds IQR. Taking the maximum of the IQR upper bound, std upper bound and 1e-6 covers distance-v1's IQR/std branch and floor. Exact masks preserve group denominators; nonnegative weights preserve component bounds; rigid price retains 55% and assigns the omitted DTW contribution zero.

Adversarial evidence: The formal falsification harness tested 1,000,000 normal, constant, near-constant, spike, extreme-scale and float16-ULP channel pairs plus all 15,625 pairs of length-three sequences over five boundary values, tiled to the exact 48-sample contract. Scaled and unscaled violations are zero at 1e-12; deterministic evidence digest is eb1f8756…f3fbe. Unit tests cover all synthetic families, independent-reference agreement, every component and total against native lower/completed distance, masks, std fallback, outward radius coverage, subnormal/ULP/max values, overflow and non-finite rejection, scalar/batch parity and tamper detection.

All-authority evidence: A deterministic 64-symbol base plus every authority neighbour was evaluated independently for all 12 frozen NASDAQ queries: 368,915 real rows. There are zero total/component excesses and zero overflow rows. Per-case pruning retention ranges from 99.520590% to 99.988226%, clearing the locked 99% floor in every case. The final run took 684.02 seconds at 549.44 MB RSS and produced evidence digest 4661e1d7…08be8. Each case binds its authority digest, benchmark fingerprint, sorted source symbols and digest of every sampled source fingerprint.

Visible correction: The first complete 687.95-second authority run passed all mathematical and retention gates but recorded only authority digests. Before promotion, the artifact schema was hardened to bind candidate-source fingerprints and the entire expensive gate was rerun; the earlier successful result was not reused as final provenance evidence.

Complete row projection: The row contains 1,097 float16 values, 41 float32 radii, three mask bytes, 12-byte episode ID, eight-byte cutoff, four-byte symbol ID and one-byte tier. It occupies 2,386 unaligned bytes and 2,432 bytes at 64-byte alignment, projecting to 8.652210 GiB for 3.82m NASDAQ rows before pack headers/dictionaries. This replaces the POC's incomplete 8.43-GiB payload estimate.

Sealed verifier and regression: The digest-recomputing CLI refuses shortened/tampered million evidence, incomplete provenance, any failed authority, unsafe value, overflow, retention below 99% or row-layout drift. Two runs produce result digest ec622691…17d5d, byte-identical machine SHA-256 1ca2280f…1e61b and contract SHA-256 e92385af…40078. The complete suite passes 193/193.

Artifacts and next: Readable evidence is config/data/analogues/m04r-quantized-bound/m04r-quantized-bound.html; expensive source evidence is under config/data/analogues/poc/m04r. Generated artifacts remain ignored. M04R-04 proves bound safety and sampled pruning only. M04R-05 must select the compact exact-aligned proposal seeder; M04R-06 must still pass physical pack I/O, capacity, corruption, resume and 1% projected-runtime gates before any full store build.

L-051 — M04R-05 compact proposal rejection and bound-assisted pivot

What was built: Added frozen exact-aligned proposal-v2 layouts at 192, 240 and 320 dimensions. Every layout carries native 48-value stage and nine-value directional structure, all nineteen exact channel masks, fixed downsampling of exact price/candle-volume/volume-shock/market fields, a projected coarse route and frozen distance-v1 weights. Scalar and vectorized batch projection are byte-identical. A multi-query scorer evaluates all 12 queries together while preserving repeated-scalar results exactly. Route quotas, storage bytes and float32/float16 behavior are contract-bound under digest dcb44ebf809fdb994b8bb7eac901f880187171778b9544986e0f20122781b969; no per-query weights or outcomes are permitted.

How it was evaluated: A resumable eight-process evaluator streamed all 11,584 A/B NASDAQ symbols once, built exact candidates from the shared kernel, and accumulated conservative global rank intervals for every route, dtype, layout, authority and top-20 target. It binds every source's causal prefix, re-hashes all 11,584 prefixes before checkpoint reuse, verifies all 240 target IDs and refreshed exact scores, and requires literal eligible-row equality with each frozen authority. Four- and eight-process 235-symbol preflights produced identical result digest 7c01b4c…2b195. The full scan took 1,918.10 seconds; the provenance-only resume revalidation took 202.05 seconds.

Compact-only result: COMPLETE/REJECT, not PASS. All 12 row counts match exactly, from 2,575,418 to 3,786,118 eligible rows. Eleven authorities are 20/20 at both 10k and 20k for every layout. Historical CAKE retains 13/20 and 16/20 for 192, 15/20 and 18/20 for 240, and 17/20 and 20/20 for 320. Because the frozen rule requires at least 19/20 at 10k and 20/20 at 20k in every case, no layout is selected. Independent verifier result b8428f95…14ebb fails for the single explicit reason no proposal layout satisfies the frozen selection rule; full evidence digest is 905ca6e3…162f3.

Precision and capacity result: Raw float16 is rejected rather than silently saturated. Depending on layout, 10–16 full-universe rows overflow; ENSC and GBSN examples reach absolute values up to 4,701,443.5. Saturated scores are diagnostic-only and produced no route-admission disagreement on the 240 targets, but maximum individual route-rank deltas reach 12,055. Float32 signatures require 771/963/1,283 bytes including masks, projecting to 2.743/3.426/4.564 GiB for 3.82m rows. Mean target exact/composite Spearman improves from 0.405 to only 0.455 as dimensions grow, corroborating the rejection.

Synthetic and implementation verification: Every morphology family passes planted-clone, invariant-positive, reversed-return and flipped-context ordering for all layouts. The planted clone remains rank one after 27 enumeration-leading same-family decoys. Tests cover masks, missing context, storage bytes, the distinct close-path share, float16 sensitivity, non-finite rejection, route quotas, row/order invariance and multi-query/scalar identity. A sealed CLI independently recomputes evidence digests, conservative admissions, selection, storage and dtype rules. The complete repository suite passes 209/209; Python compilation, whitespace checks and HTML parsing succeed.

Bound-assisted decision POC: The compact failure was preserved, then a separate fixed allocation reserved 10% of each pool for the native certified lower-bound route, 9% for each compact component and 27% for compact composite. Re-reading all 12 full frontiers shows every authority target has native-bound rank ≤56. Consequently all layouts retain 20/20 in all cases even at pool 1,000. Two complete executions took 316.30 and 241.74 seconds and produced the identical evidence digest 4e5369b6…59a45. This is recall evidence for a bound-first packed POC, not a compact-v2 promotion.

Boundary and next: Do not build a compact-v2 store and do not lower the selection rule. M04R-06 must first confirm all-12 ranks using the actual quantized/error-corrected bound, add a proved sidecar/fallback for full-universe float16 overflows, and measure cold/warm 1% packed bound-first performance against an optional bound-assisted 192 route. The full 30 GiB shadow-build and ≤11 GiB active-store constraints remain in force.

L-052 — M04R-06A full-universe quantized-bound rank certification

Why: M04R-05B's native float64 frontier ranks were encouraging but could not justify a packed architecture: storage quantization can weaken a lower bound, and rare rows exceed the frozen int16 domain. The next decision therefore required ranks from the actual error-corrected quantized representation over the complete universe, not an estimate or sampled proxy.

What and how: Added a resumable eight-process census that loads all 12 frozen authorities, revalidates their causal query prefixes, reconstructs every eligible exact row through the common feature kernel, quantizes it under contract 692e5f40…0d1, and scores all queries together. Process-isolated chunks return additive rank counters; the parent merges them in source order, rejects duplicate symbols, binds all 11,584 stock-prefix digests plus the shared candidate benchmark prefix, verifies all 240 targets and requires literal eligible-row equality. Resume re-hashes every completed stock prefix and benchmark prefix before trusting a checkpoint. Forward outcomes are never loaded.

Result: PASS for the rank preflight. All 12 authority row totals match (2.575m–3.786m per query) and every target is seen once. Native-bound maximum rank 56 becomes conservative quantized-bound maximum rank 343. Top-100 therefore fails as a diagnostic, but all 240 targets are inside top 500 and the predeclared hybrid top-1,000 quota passes. Full evidence digest is 95a17cdc6dd47b4a6fa511572d869d76100ce0936034259d8c87184688bcc179.

Overflow safety: Thirty-five full-universe rows exceed the certified int16 quantizer. No value is saturated and no candidate disappears. Each such row emits no quantized record, receives the universal safe routing bound zero for every query, and is required in an exact/float32 sidecar. This can reduce pruning performance but cannot cause a false prune. The sidecar's physical encoding, integrity and lookup behavior remain an M04R-06B gate.

Independent verification and cost: The sealed CLI recomputes the deterministic evidence digest, stock-plus-benchmark source-scope digest, unique authority/target identities, tie-interval arithmetic, finite bound values, recalls at 100/500/1,000/2,000, maximum ranks, authority row accounting, overflow examples/policy and the final gate. It passes with digest 847b2a9636fca04fce6d08f05c0a18facebe4a3238dc9f93160e1edc62e9c1b2. The full raw-OHLCV reconstruction/quantization/scoring run took 2,069.33 seconds; the final stock-and-benchmark provenance-only resume took 157.75 seconds. These timings include feature reconstruction and are not evidence that a packed scan meets its latency target.

Boundary and next: This certifies development-target retention by the quantized route, not full search correctness or production performance. M04R-06B must now build the physical 1% immutable pack plus overflow sidecar, prove byte-level integrity/resume/corruption behavior, and measure cold/warm scan time, RSS and full-store capacity projections. Only a passing pack can authorize a 100% build.

L-053 — M04R-06B immutable 1% packed-store gate

What was built: Added a content-addressed immutable generation format under contract 9af266e24c0b539243e70c250c3c88f512c6e913a4ef9b1ada050016f0175dbf. Its explicit little-endian 2,432-byte record contains raw 12-byte episode ID, nanosecond cutoff, uint32 symbol ID, tier, three presence bytes, every certified float16 field and all 41 outward float32 error radii. A separate 32-byte sidecar record carries overflow identity/metadata only: its routing bound is always zero and native exact scoring must reconstruct it from the digest-bound source. Values are never clipped or saturated.

Generation safety: Each generation binds file byte counts/SHA-256, row and quantized-bound contracts, symbol dictionary, selection, all selected stock prefixes, the shared benchmark prefix and the M04R-06A evidence digest. Readers reject stale expected provenance, unsafe paths/IDs, changed manifests, missing/truncated/corrupt files, noncanonical order, duplicate episodes, invalid tiers, negative radii, non-finite values, nonzero padding and unused presence bits. Activation is an atomic digest-bound pointer replacement and old generations remain rollback targets. Tests show incremental old-plus-append generation equals a clean rebuild.

Real 1% construction: The fixed sample contains ceil(11,584 × 1%) = 116 symbols. Known overflow-example symbols are forced inside that fixed count so the physical sidecar cannot pass vacuously; remaining symbols follow a frozen SHA-256 order. An intentional run stopped after 20 symbols with no active generation, then resumed to all 116. It produced 37,083 main rows and 22 real overflow rows, 37,105 total, occupying 90,186,560 bytes. A clean serial build and resumed eight-process build produce the identical generation 27ce3af2d9aa9193e3771b9367c41d272e4202df34bf17617a7ec25576a0958b.

Correctness and performance: Array-backed mmap scoring equals object/scalar quantized scoring exactly in differential tests. Two passes across all 12 frozen queries produce identical top-1,000 score digests. Each query is projected by its own authority eligible-row count divided by observed eligible sample rows, replacing and rejecting an initially optimistic fixed 100× shortcut. Final eight-process evidence projects 248.56 seconds worst warm and 227.94 seconds after an advised cache eviction, with 615.81 MB peak parent RSS. The projected 3.82m-row active store is 8.6523 GiB. Evidence digests are 2d082616…17ecc parallel and 2f85e6fe…df2a4 serial; independent verifier digest ed1c9e59dd8e3b64ae3cb243c219acbf3ccc919a378131f3ca9a9f19895b0a9f passes.

Alternatives and boundary: Raw mmap with a 2,048-row compiled NumPy working block is selected. Sweeps at 1,024/4,096/8,192 rows keep identical results and offer no material speed benefit; Blosc2 is not installed. Adding proposal-v2/192 float32 would add 2.7429 GiB and bring the active projection to 11.3952 GiB before extra metadata, failing the 11 GiB limit; compact-only retrieval had already failed M04R-05. This 1% gate authorizes M04R-06C's 100% immutable build. It does not yet claim a full-store scan, certified final search, outcome validation or production promotion.

L-054 — M04R-06C full immutable shadow generation

Build and provenance: The immediate precheck found 34,299,658,240 free bytes (31.94 GiB), above the frozen 30 GiB threshold. The builder intentionally stopped after 100/11,584 symbols, then resumed through digest/size/source-prefix validation. A streaming publisher concatenated ordered per-symbol shards without a pack-sized RAM copy and atomically published generation 9fc6ae0ec4451133d8006897162f3443803fd30a3c44b78e80a476fbb18bb483. No active.json was created. The generation binds every stock causal prefix, the benchmark prefix, M04R-06A rank evidence, symbol dictionary, contracts and file hashes.

Physical result: The shadow has 3,786,121 quantized records and 35 metadata-only exact-source fallbacks, 3,786,156 total. Main plus sidecar occupy 9,207,847,392 bytes (8.5755 GiB); the frozen 3.82m projection is 8.6522 GiB. Its manifest is 3.67 MB. Each of the 12 metadata eligibility counts exactly matches its authority, including 2,575,418 minimum and 3,786,118 maximum. The 35 sidecar rows reappear physically, route at zero and cannot be falsely pruned.

Full-scale failures kept visible: Raw mmap looked acceptable at 1%, but touching the full file left about 9.1 GiB of file-backed pages resident, violating the 1 GiB scan-process gate. Per-block MADV_DONTNEED did not reliably release them. The scorer therefore switched to bounded positional reads into 2,048-row buffers while preserving the identical raw pack and bound kernel. A subsequent completed three-scan run reported a false 9,289 MB child peak because multiprocessing inherited the validator's historical ru_maxrss; that failed artifact is preserved with digest 5a3f7e0490378bd58dcbc6cb2e7c22cb9a69dbe96d0c3e2209dea735e99e03d7. A post-scan payload NameError then forced another full retry and received a direct regression test. None of these runs was promoted.

Final scan gate: The worst query is not selected after seeing full timings; it is digest-bound as the maximum query-specific warm projection from sealed 1% evidence 2d082616…17ecc. Query cb2bdccd1386790ef8a16bd0 scans all 3,786,118 eligible rows in 378.83 seconds advised-cold, 264.55 seconds warm and 269.66 seconds repeated warm. All three produce top-1,000 digest fc088e4c40cbff3b6d129951f16eb79abf7575a129d8fbc3cd7dfd835bc763fa. Per-block current RSS peaks at 241.21 MB. Thus cold ≤600, warm ≤300, RSS ≤1 GiB, capacity ≤11 GiB, determinism and row-accounting gates pass.

Verification and cleanup: Final builder evidence digest is 43e7246ac20e35fe696fe816764e102e4f04472ba3eb3b36fa09615dee42662f. The separate verifier re-hashes the 9.2 GB files and independently checks shadow state, all provenance, physical counts, all 12 authorities, worst-query selection, timing/RSS mathematics and retry output; it passes with digest 08a6f62fde5738dbfb14e46c9ee97faca87cad714f9e659110cc2ac02f11250a. After passing, only the 8.7-GB rebuildable packed-bound-full/work checkpoint directory was deleted; generation and evidence remain. Free space returned to 24 GB.

Boundary and next: This is a verified shadow lower-bound store, not a production search replacement. M04R-07 must implement globally stable bounded proposal selection over it; M04R-08 must prove exact certified completion and final constrained top-k equality before activation. Forward outcomes remain untouched.

L-055 — M04R-07 global bounded proposal integration

Why: The full shadow generation proved storage and score-only scanning, but it did not return episode identities or remove the old correctness-critical per-symbol truncation. M04R-07 needed a real globally bounded candidate handoff before exact completion could be designed.

What and how: Added contract m04r-global-bound-proposal-v1, digest 059db6d78bbe62e7588e1fe2408fe244040b501d190bdf7b211a7ecd7aadb96c. It applies tier, cutoff, exact query-ID and same-symbol overlap eligibility before scoring; reads fixed blocks with pread; maintains bounded deterministic selections with the complete 96-bit episode ID as the tie key; and preserves all metadata-only overflow rows at universal bound zero. The composite top-1,000 is the certified, non-displacing route. Seven 64-row coarse/component routes are unioned as optional exact-threshold seeds and can never evict a composite candidate.

Plan correction from evidence: The original task named an exact-weighted compact and legacy topology route. M04R-05 rejected compact-only recall, and M04R-06B measured that the smallest valid separate compact row would push the store to 11.3952 GiB. Those rejected fields were not reintroduced. Only composite/coarse/stage/price/candle-volatility/volume-shock/market-context/structural values computed from the selected certified pack are routed; only composite carries all-authority recall evidence.

Adversarial verification: Tests force physical order to disagree with 96-bit ID tie order, vary blocks from one row upward, reverse scan order, exercise IDs containing zero bytes, preserve exact-source overflow, reject future/query-overlap/wrong-tier rows before ranking, forbid composite quotas below 1,000 and reject unsupported routes. Results remain identical with more than 25 same-symbol decoys and tied near-clones.

Full result: The preregistered M04R-06C worst query scans 3,786,156 physical rows and 3,786,118 eligible rows. Forward 2,048-row and reverse 4,097-row passes return the identical 1,170-candidate digest c8f51e202205367bf3cfda6a62cd3bcbe0e29e32f5b809d7e88431a194692555. Composite contains exactly 1,000 rows, retains all 20 selected exact neighbours, and reproduces earlier score digest fc088e4c40cbff3b6d129951f16eb79abf7575a129d8fbc3cd7dfd835bc763fa. All-12 sealed rank evidence independently places every 240 target within composite quota (maximum conservative rank 343).

Performance and verifier: Full passes take 263.106 and 263.320 seconds at 279.73 MiB peak RSS, below 600 seconds and 768 MiB. Evidence digest is 5147c1a15419df196ccd1936c1cc43c29641461a91369cef6f824aa8e7d42d13. The separate verifier re-hashes the 9.2 GB generation, checks shadow/provenance/build/rank bindings, recomputes row/target/route/gate logic and passes with digest 4f5db61b95c460126ef923fd1607de636837a1c87df43b435b6540a0d99cb635.

Boundary and next: Proposal recall on development authorities is now repaired, but lower-bound candidates are not final nearest neighbours. M04R-08 must native-exact-score seeds, scan every eligible lower bound, safely prune only above the live constrained threshold, group unresolved source reconstruction, reproduce exact authorities and emit a complete stopping/exhaustion certificate. The generation remains shadow-only and outcomes remain untouched.

L-056 — M04R-08A certified completion kernel and first full query

Correctness architecture: Added m04r-certified-packed-search-v1. It binds query stock/benchmark causal prefixes, request scope, packed provenance and generation; selects a global bound prefix plus the first omitted row; adaptively reconstructs native candidates by symbol; verifies every quantized bound is no greater than its recomputed native bound; applies the existing overlap/per-instrument constrained selector; and returns only when the selected top-k is full and the first omitted bound is strictly greater than kth exact distance, or the eligible universe is exhausted. If the frontier ceiling is insufficient it fails closed without emitting matches.

Synthetic differential: A physical content-addressed market-agnostic pack is searched with different block sizes and worker counts. Both certificates are byte-stable apart from elapsed time and match brute-force IDs and native distances exactly. Accounting requires exact + safely pruned = eligible; prefix, identity, eligibility and quantized/native-bound checks execute during reconstruction.

Rejected first full attempt: The initial implementation treated all 8,192 retained bound rows as mandatory exact work. On the preregistered worst query it was still running at roughly 14 minutes and about 843 MiB RSS, so it was interrupted with exit 130. This exceeds the 600-second maximum and is retained here as a failed design result.

Correction and measured result: The engine now exact-scores 512 stable bound seeds first, derives a finite constrained τ, then materializes only unscored frontier rows whose safe bound is ≤τ. The same full query certifies in 409.1715 seconds at 546.00 MiB. It scores 2,407 of 3,786,118 eligible rows, safely prunes 3,783,711, uses 1,525 grouped materialization operations (1,522 sparse, three batch), and stops at 0.7993423150 > 0.7735697168. Ordered IDs exactly equal all 20 frozen authority IDs. Certificate digest is 664bcb954590df46a72634d47dd1fee086e3b5845e6604ca852b971f01fea9cb.

Boundary and next: This is M04R-08A development evidence, not the complete gate. The worst query passes the 600-second/1,536-MiB maximum but exceeds the 300-second p95 ambition. Next persist the failed/successful runs through a verifier-owned experiment, measure exact numeric/component/alignment tolerances, repeat deterministically, run all 12 authorities, then implement shared-I/O batch mode. The shadow generation remains inactive and outcomes remain untouched.

L-057 — M04R-08A repeated full certification and independent verifier

Why: One successful development execution was insufficient: it did not persist durable machine evidence, test different execution controls, quantify the post-refactor authority tolerance, preserve the rejected design in the same gate or protect against a self-asserted pass.

What and how: Added a checkpointing full-universe gate that binds the preregistered worst query, frozen authority, packed-build digest, physical generation and certified-search contract. It runs at least twice with distinct block/worker settings, records partial state after each completed run, fails visibly on exceptions, excludes timing from the deterministic evidence digest and never reads forward outcomes or setup labels. Added a separate verifier that re-hashes the 9.2-GB generation, validates shadow state and authority/build integrity, independently reconstructs each certificate digest from matches and float hex values, and recomputes repeat, numeric, stopping, accounting and resource gates.

Full evidence: Runs using 2,048 rows/eight workers and 4,097 rows/four workers complete in 417.1000 and 509.1447 seconds at 558.52 MiB peak. Both return the exact 20 frozen IDs and alignments, evaluate 2,407 of 3,786,118 eligible candidates, prune 3,783,711 safely, and stop at lower bound 0.7993423150 above τ 0.7735697168. Both reconstruct certificate digest 664bcb954590df46a72634d47dd1fee086e3b5845e6604ca852b971f01fea9cb. Maximum differences from the older authority are 7.8538e−9 total and 9.8172e−8 component, isolated to the pre-shared-kernel coarse float path and accepted under explicit 1e−7/1e−6 practical development tolerances while IDs/alignments remain exact.

Failures and verifier: The evidence retains the first 8,192-row exact-all attempt as an interrupted performance failure at 840 seconds, 843 MiB and exit 130. Gate evidence digest is 8382e417a4d528ccaa423664180a697e5dd42f767968d139db61094e88f48647. Independent physical verification passes with digest 592ecba94b9e8c4b0c01a1cc55f8c8ac6a08a88676fe748ea4c27c980490a070. A threaded optional-volume stage exposed a benign nanmean empty-slice warning caused by process-global warning-filter races; the operation now uses an explicit finite count and divide, proven bit-identical and warning-free across 64 concurrent calls.

Boundary and next: M04R-08A is complete only for the preregistered worst development query. It meets the 600-second maximum but not the 300-second p95 ambition. Next run all 12 frozen NASDAQ authorities with checkpoint/resume and aggregate gates, then implement shared-I/O multi-query batching and prove result/certificate equality plus material total-time reduction. The pack remains shadow-only.

L-058 — M04R-08B resumable all-authority runner preflight

Why: Twelve independent full scans can take about an hour and must survive interruption without trusting partial output or silently rescoring completed cases. The matrix also needs a single explicit p95/max/resource decision rather than twelve informal console observations.

What and how: Added a sequential NASDAQ all-12 runner that re-hashes the immutable shadow generation before work, validates all frozen authority/build digests, uses deterministic query-ID order, atomically writes one complete case at a time, and rewrites aggregate JSON/HTML after every case. Resume accepts a case only after independently recomputing its evidence digest, authority comparison, exact certificate digest, bindings and all case gates. Timing and RSS are reported but excluded from stable case/matrix digests; their pass/fail booleans remain bound. Exceptions are recorded and later authorities continue.

Unit/adversarial checks: Checkpoint tampering is rejected even when the outer case digest is recomputed. Changing timestamps, raw timings and RSS does not change the deterministic aggregate digest. Partial matrices remain visibly incomplete. Existing certificate, verifier and CLI focused tests continue to pass.

Real preflight: First sorted authority 076c8996…c9bc (SLE, 29 May 2024) exactly matches its frozen IDs/order/alignments, certifies after only the 512 seed evaluations in 263.5374 seconds and records 397.42 MiB process high-water RSS. A new process performs the physical hash, verifies and skips this checkpoint without rescoring; matrix digest remains 19361f97f1085b5dceedfce4a9b313efc0224dfb33150c587ae442f59d6fb890.

Boundary and next: This is a runner/resume pass, not M04R-08B completion. Execute the remaining eleven authorities, independently verify the aggregate, and accept M04R-08B only if every case is exact, maximum ≤600 seconds, p95 ≤300 seconds and RSS ≤1,536 MiB. Do not weaken a failed performance limit.

L-059 — M04R-08B all-12 composite-seed baseline rejected

What completed: The committed resumable runner processed all 12 frozen NASDAQ development authorities, checkpointing each complete case and producing matrix evidence digest f91bac4da70d8ed90939f416acd9898fc17133197bcad5d72a998b010fe644a6. Total serial certified-search time was 5,112.1061 seconds.

Correctness result: All 12 cases reproduce exact authority IDs, order and alignments. Maximum total/component deltas remain below 1e−7/1e−6; every stored quantized bound is safe; every certificate has complete eligible = exact + pruned accounting and a strict first-omitted-bound stop. There were no runner exceptions. Peak process high-water RSS is 975.55 MiB, below 1,536 MiB.

Performance failure: Frozen p95/max limits are 300/600 seconds. Observed p95 is 832.4444 seconds and maximum 984.2781 seconds. Historical TVTX exact-scores 9,309 candidates across 3,937 materialization groups in 984.28 seconds; historical CAKE scores 7,783 in 708.22; current TAIT scores 7,419 in 687.48. The other nine range from 243.87 to 423.84 seconds. The baseline is rejected; limits are not changed.

Independent verification: Added a verifier that re-hashes the physical shadow generation; validates all build, authority, case, input and certificate bindings; reconstructs certificate/case/matrix digests; recomputes all numeric, correctness and resource gates; and distinguishes authentic rejected evidence from verifier failure. It reports evidence verified, design rejected, no integrity failures, result digest aa6da7632d3e43e6ec6285e55fc0749e98a52946ee17c41516445e261bf979ff. A synthetic test alters a match and recomputes outer digests; independent certificate/authority checks still reject it.

Diagnosis and next: Heavy cases receive a loose threshold from the first 512 composite-bound seeds, forcing 7.4k–9.3k exact evaluations. M04R-07 already computes a non-displacing component-route union specifically for τ seeding, but M04R-08A currently requests only composite rows. Freeze this baseline, POC component-route seeding on the three heavy cases, and adopt a new contract only if exact results remain unchanged and measured runtime materially improves under the original gates.

L-060 — Heavy-completion diagnosis and requested-position v2 POC

Seed hypothesis rejected: Additional component-route seeds cannot improve the heavy cases. All 240 authority targets have conservative composite rank at most 343, while completion already exact-scores 512 seeds. Historical TVTX, historical CAKE and current TAIT each retain one identical constrained τ from the first 512-score round through final certification. Their 7.4k–9.3k refinements are forced by the existing bound, not by a poor initial result threshold.

Measured batch amplification: A bounded TVTX profiler re-scanned the immutable pack in 228.42 seconds, exactly reproduced all 9,309 baseline refinement rows and grouped them into 2,839 unique symbols. Under the current eight-row switch, 220 combined groups containing only 2,195 requested cutoffs would construct 194,505 five-session historical windows—88.61× amplification and 192,310 irrelevant representations. Metadata for all groups loads in 25.17 seconds with 16 threads. Profile evidence digest is b2da6643…c0b8.

POC implementation: Added a vector constructor that takes explicit cutoff positions, aligns the benchmark once per symbol, extracts only the requested OHLCV windows and feeds the shared exact channel/materialization body. Existing sliding behavior is preserved. Differential tests cover unordered positions, full/partial/missing benchmark context, scalar and sliding references, invalid/duplicate positions, and certified v1/v2 result equivalence. On 5,397 real NVDA rows with 14 requested cutoffs, it takes 0.0480 seconds versus 1.3864 seconds for all 1,030 sliding windows, a 28.87× speedup with exact checked core arrays.

Versioning and next: The optimization is explicit m04r-certified-packed-search-v2; v1 remains unchanged so the rejected baseline is reproducible. The resumable runner can select exact query IDs and writes v2 evidence separately. Next run only the three frozen heavy cases. Do not run a new all-12 matrix unless those cases retain exact IDs/totals/components/alignments and fall below 600 seconds with a credible path toward 300-second p95.

L-061 — Pure requested-position v2 rejected; hybrid v3 prepared

What happened: The first frozen heavy authority, historical TVTX, completed under pure requested-position v2 with the unchanged 9,309 exact evaluations and exact authority result, but took 778.76 seconds. This improves the v1 baseline of 984.28 seconds yet still fails the unchanged 600-second maximum. The run was stopped before CAKE and TAIT because the proposed variant had already failed its admission gate; its atomic TVTX checkpoint and incomplete matrix remain preserved under config/data/analogues/poc/m04r/certified-packed-search-v2-heavy.

Why: Requested-position batching removed dense-group full-history amplification, but v2 also sent thousands of one-to-seven-row symbol groups through a per-symbol vector path that aligns the complete benchmark frame. That replaced the already efficient scalar sparse path and consumed much of the gain. This is a measured implementation mismatch, not a reason to relax the performance target.

How v3 differs: Added a separately versioned m04r-certified-packed-search-v3 contract: groups below the existing eight-proposal cutoff retain exact scalar reconstruction, while dense groups construct only their requested cutoffs. v1 and pure v2 remain unchanged and reproducible. The runner records mutually exclusive mode controls, rejects ambiguous combinations and binds checkpoints to the selected contract digest. Physical-pack differential tests prove v3 equality for IDs, totals, components, paths, input digest and exact-evaluation count; the full test suite passes.

Next: Run TVTX alone under v3. Continue to CAKE and TAIT only if TVTX stays exact and falls below 600 seconds. A credible all-12 rerun additionally requires a measured route toward the frozen 300-second p95; otherwise profile the remaining scan/exact split before changing another design variable.

L-062 — Hybrid v3 rejected; sparse-path hypothesis corrected

Measured result: Historical TVTX under hybrid v3 is exactly equal to its authority—same ordered IDs, components within 1.12e−7, exact alignments, 9,309 evaluations and strict certificate—but takes 857.84 seconds at 696.49 MiB. It improves v1 by 126.44 seconds but is 79.08 seconds slower than pure requested-position v2. Therefore v3 fails the unchanged 600-second maximum and CAKE/TAIT were not launched.

Correction: The prior attribution of v2's remaining time to sparse-group vector overhead is falsified. Across identical completion rounds, v2 is faster than the hybrid that restores scalar sparse reconstruction. All three variants certify only after two physical scans: frontier 8,192 cannot stop, then frontier 16,384 reaches the final 9,309 exact rows. The next isolated variable is scan duplication, not another reconstruction guess.

Next POC: The runner now exposes and records initial_frontier_rows, validates its bounds, and refuses to resume a checkpoint whose complete controls differ from the requested run. Test pure v2 at an initial 16,384 frontier so TVTX performs one pack scan. The scoring contract is unchanged; certificate rounds and evidence controls capture the execution difference. Accept only exact authority equality and ≤600 seconds, then evaluate whether the result leaves a credible ≤300-second p95 route.

L-063 — One-scan pure v2 passes TVTX maximum gate

Result: Pure requested-position v2 with initial frontier 16,384 completes historical TVTX in one physical scan and passes every case gate. It returns the exact frozen 20 IDs/order and alignments, maximum total/component deltas 8.91e−9/1.12e−7, 9,309 exact + 3,003,508 safely pruned = 3,012,817 eligible, and strict next bound 1.0719771 > τ 1.0523244. Runtime is 569.59 seconds and peak RSS 614.12 MiB. Case/certificate digests are d80b10d4…6ad5/4521cc73…8488.

Comparison: Holding the pure-v2 scoring path fixed, removing the second full-pack scan saves 209.17 seconds versus the 8,192-start v2 run. It saves 414.69 seconds versus v1. The one-case aggregate correctly remains incomplete because the frozen matrix gate requires all 12 cases; this does not negate the individual TVTX PASS.

Boundary and next: The ≤600-second maximum now has a passing route, but 569.59 seconds is far above the ≤300-second p95 objective. Run historical CAKE and current TAIT under the same v2/16,384 controls to establish whether every former maximum failure is repaired. Do not promote or launch a full all-12 rerun yet; next profile shared pack I/O and multi-query completion using these results, because another single-query scalar tweak lacks evidence of reaching p95.

L-064 — All three former maximum failures repaired; p95 remains rejected

Heavy-case results: Under identical pure-v2, initial-16,384, 2,048-block, eight-worker controls, historical CAKE completes in 425.75 seconds with 7,783 exact rows and current TAIT in 560.92 seconds with 7,419. Together with TVTX at 569.59 seconds/9,309 rows, all three exactly match their frozen authorities and pass the unchanged 600-second case maximum and 1,536-MiB RSS limit. CAKE improves by 282.47 seconds and TAIT by 126.56 seconds over v1.

Aggregate interpretation: The intentionally partial CAKE/TAIT matrix is 2/12 and therefore not a matrix PASS. Its p95 is 554.16 seconds, which confirms that independent per-query scanning still violates the frozen 300-second p95 even after all former hard maximum failures are repaired. No full all-12 rerun is authorized from these partial results.

Next: Implement M04R-08C as a bounded shared-I/O POC: read each physical pack block once for multiple queries, preserve query-specific eligibility, bounded heaps, τ, exact completion and certificates, and prove byte-identical proposal/certificate results against repeated scalar calls. Measure physical rows/bytes read, shared wall time, per-query completion time and RSS. Retain it only if total elapsed materially falls; otherwise profile/decompose the quantized-bound kernel before another design change.

L-065 — M04R-08C shared proposal-scan preflight

Implementation: Added a market-agnostic batch proposal API that validates a non-empty unique ordered query set, loads the immutable generation once, performs one positional read per physical block, and feeds that block to independent per-query eligibility, bound and bounded-heap states. Each embedded report retains the scalar schema, candidate/result digests and complete eligible-row accounting; the batch wrapper separately records physical rows scanned, logical query-row evaluations, shared elapsed/RSS and a contract-bound digest.

Verification: On a physical packed fixture, forward 17-row and reverse 64-row batch scans are byte-equivalent to independent scalar scans performed with 31-row blocks for both a broad A/B query and a same-symbol/A-only temporal query. Candidate tuples, candidate digests, scalar result digests, eligibility and route counts all match. One 1,038-row physical scan accounts for 2,076 logical rows across two queries. Empty and duplicate query sets fail closed; the full test suite passes.

Next: Run one shared composite-quota-16,385 scan for the three frozen heavy authorities and compare every per-query candidate digest/eligible count to the already certified one-scan v2 checkpoints. Measure shared elapsed and RSS. This POC covers proposal I/O only; even a pass does not yet share exact source reconstruction or emit new final-search certificates.

L-066 — Shared physical-read scan rejected on measured speed

Correctness: The full 3,786,156-row immutable pack was scanned for TVTX, CAKE and TAIT in one shared traversal. All three embedded reports exactly match same-process scalar candidates, candidate/result digests, eligible counts and route counts, and reproduce the candidate digests already bound into their certified final-search checkpoints. Physical/logical accounting is 3,786,156/11,358,468 rows; peak RSS is 247.71 MiB. Every correctness/resource gate passes.

Performance rejection: Shared elapsed is 626.34 seconds. Three scalar controls in the same process take 205.36, 180.79 and 255.55 seconds, totaling 641.70. The measured gain is only 1.0245×/2.39%, below the preregistered 10% material threshold, so evidence digest 2e042acf…a49 is a FAIL and the batch wrapper is not promoted into certified final search.

Meaning and next: Reading each block once is correct but physical I/O is not the dominant cost; query-specific quantized-bound statistics dominate. Preserve the batch primitive as tested infrastructure, but do not claim it repairs p95. Next decompose the real bound kernel on fixed pack rows, measuring eligibility, presence unpacking/conversion, coarse/stage/structure, each 48-sample group, quantile/std denominators and heap maintenance. Test compact alternatives against identical float results before changing the production kernel.

L-067 — Quantized-kernel profile and combined-IQR POC

Profile: Five in-memory calls over the same 32,768 real TVTX-eligible packed rows take 11.227 seconds. NumPy percentile consumes 7.348 seconds/65.5% and its partition primitive alone 6.702 seconds; standard deviation is 1.261 seconds, dtype conversion 0.940 and presence unpacking negligible. This confirms the shared-read rejection: query-dependent denominator statistics, especially IQR, dominate.

Verified compact change: The existing vector kernel separately called percentile at 25 and 75 for every present 48-sample channel, partitioning the same joined 96-value row twice. It now requests (25, 75) in one call and subtracts the returned quartiles. A call-count regression proves one combined request per present channel; all independent-reference, safety, missingness, float16-boundary, packed-store and proposal tests pass, followed by the full suite.

Measured result and next: On the identical fixed rows, median kernel time falls from 2.2454 to 1.8246 seconds per call, a 1.2306×/18.7% elapsed reduction with unchanged checked outputs. This is an in-memory kernel POC, not yet a full-search performance pass. Commit it separately, then run a full TVTX composite scan and require the exact prior candidate digest/eligible accounting plus material elapsed reduction before rerunning certified completion.

L-068 — Combined-IQR full TVTX proposal scan PASS

Full-pack result: The optimized kernel scans all 3,786,156 physical rows for historical TVTX with composite quota 16,385 and returns the exact frozen candidate digest 2444aac7…5ed7, 3,012,817 eligible rows and 16,385 candidates. Result digest is dfd7f7c3…8aa1; peak RSS is 219.35 MiB.

Performance: Elapsed is 162.76 seconds versus 205.36 seconds for the same-process scalar control recorded immediately before the change, a 42.59-second/20.74% reduction. This materially confirms the fixed-row 1.2306× kernel result at complete-pack scale without altering proposal identity or eligibility.

Next: Rerun one-scan pure-v2 certified TVTX from a fresh checkpoint. Require exact authority IDs/order/totals/components/alignments, 9,309 exact evaluations, strict stopping and unchanged resource gates. This scan improvement alone cannot be assumed to reach the 300-second p95 because source reconstruction/exact distance remains outside the optimized kernel.

L-069 — Combined-IQR certified TVTX PASS; exact completion now dominates

End-to-end result: One-scan pure-v2 TVTX with the combined-IQR bound kernel exactly reproduces the frozen authority and prior deterministic evidence: 9,309 exact rows, 3,003,508 safely pruned, unchanged IDs/order/totals/components/alignments, strict stopping and all case gates PASS. Runtime is 508.08 seconds at 607.06 MiB.

Comparison: The optimized run saves 61.50 seconds/10.8% versus one-scan pre-change v2 at 569.59 seconds and 476.20 seconds/48.4% versus v1 at 984.28. The deterministic partial-matrix digest remains 7e613120…372 because timing fields are deliberately excluded and results/certificates are unchanged. The one-case matrix remains incomplete by design.

Boundary and next: TVTX still exceeds the 300-second p95 target by 208.08 seconds. Its full bound scan is now 162.76 seconds, leaving roughly 345 seconds in source validation, requested-position reconstruction, exact distance/DTW, selection and overhead. Profile that exact-completion path by phase and group density on the frozen 9,309 proposals before changing it. Do not rerun CAKE/TAIT or all 12 yet.

L-070 — Certified exact-completion profile and vector-bound decision

Profile validity: The standalone profiler reconstructs the same composite frontier, 512 seed rows, 8,797 pending rows, 9,309 total exact evaluations, threshold, strict stop and final frozen top-20 IDs as optimized TVTX. It passes with evidence digest 9ef1f1fb…1c2b. Optimized scan wall time is 164.10 seconds and exact completion wall time 355.11.

Worker-time decomposition: Across eight overlapping workers, requested representation construction consumes 942.14 cumulative seconds, native non-DTW lower-bound distance 935.95, source loads 411.76, full distance/DTW 296.42, causal-prefix validation 162.20 and eligibility 7.89. Sparse/dense group totals are 2,481.08/355.99 seconds. DTW is only 10.8% of measured phase time; representation construction and the scalar native lower bound are the primary targets.

Existing capability found: batch_representation_lower_bounds already implements the same exact 48-sample native bound vectorized over representation groups and has independent differential verification, but certified completion does not call it. A bounded benchmark over group sizes 1/2/4/8/16/32/64 measures 1.09×/2.08×/3.99×/7.09×/12.15×/18.89×/26.17× speedups with maximum total delta 1.11e−16.

Next: Add this path under a distinct certified-search contract because last-bit values may change even inside the frozen practical tolerance; v1–v3 remain reproducible. Differentially require IDs/order/alignments exact, totals/components within 1e−12 on fixtures, bound safety and unchanged exact accounting, then run optimized TVTX only.

L-071 — Vector native-bound v4 preflight

Implementation: Added explicit m04r-certified-packed-search-v4. It retains pure requested-position representation construction, then applies the existing independently verified batch_representation_lower_bounds once per symbol group before scalar DTW completion. The v4 option requires requested positions and is bound into runner controls/checkpoint validation; invalid combinations fail closed. v1–v3 code paths and contract digests remain reproducible.

Differential gate: On the market-agnostic physical packed fixture, v4 returns the same constrained IDs, order and alignments as v1–v3 and brute force, with totals/components within 1e−12, identical input digest and exact-evaluation count, plus a distinct v4 schema/contract digest. The full test suite passes after CLI/import checks.

L-072 — Vector native-bound v4 TVTX PASS; still above p95

Result: Optimized one-scan TVTX under v4 passes every authority, accounting, strict-stop, quantized-safety, 600-second and RSS case gate. It evaluates the unchanged 9,309 rows, returns exact IDs/order/alignments with the same 8.91e−9/1.12e−7 authority deltas, and completes in 468.41 seconds at 643.19 MiB. L-073 — Exact timestamp lookup retained; structural kernel isolated

Alignment POC: Replaced full pandas merge inside exact-batch benchmark-close extraction with indexed exact-timestamp lookup while preserving missing values and last-duplicate-wins semantics. Across 256 real NASDAQ frames/438,502 rows, lookup is bit-identical and 37.82× faster than merge. Across 112 real symbols/335 complete 252-bar requested representations, the full construction gain is only 1.051× with zero core-array delta, proving alignment is not the dominant representation cost.

Verification: Added an independent merge-semantics regression covering duplicates and missing timestamps. Context, exact-batch, representation, view and certified physical-pack focused tests pass, followed by the full suite. The helper also keeps the public aligned context frame behavior unchanged.

Next hotspot: A 50-repeat profile of seven real 252-bar positions attributes 0.938/1.822 seconds (51.5%) to _structural_rows, versus channel construction 0.345, resampling 0.160 and robust-z 0.158. The structural routine performs 756 sequential Python time-step iterations per symbol group while vectorizing only across its usually tiny row count. Build an exact-reference compiled/alternative POC, test threshold-boundary behavior and retain it only with exact or explicitly versioned practical parity plus material speed.

L-074 — Compiled exact structural-row kernel preflight

Implementation: Added Numba as an explicit runtime dependency and retained the original NumPy/Python structural-event implementation as an executable reference. The production wrapper performs the identical finite/NaN close-path transform, then calls a cached, no-fast-math, GIL-free compiled loop with the same .03/.06/.12 thresholds, high/low transition order, comparison inclusivity, event amplitude and duration formulas.

Correctness: Compiled and reference arrays are bit-identical on 64 random 252-bar paths, constants, all-NaN paths and exact threshold/reversal boundaries; maximum delta is zero. Existing exact-batch, scalar representation, quantized-bound and certified-search focused tests pass, followed by the full suite.

Performance: Warm group speedups are 6,389×/4,804×/3,171×/1,959× at 1/2/4/8 rows and remain 157× at 64, eliminating tiny-array Python-loop overhead. On the fixed 112-real-symbol/335-representation benchmark, complete requested representation construction falls from 2.9302 to 0.8828 seconds, a 3.319× speedup with zero core-array delta.

Next: Rerun only optimized TVTX v4 from a fresh checkpoint. JIT warming occurs during query representation before threaded completion; require exact authority equality, unchanged 9,309 accounting, strict stop and resource gates. Do not infer the end-to-end result from the microbenchmark.

L-075 — Compiled structural TVTX PASS; deferred-path omission found

TVTX result: Optimized v4 with compiled structural features passes exact authority/accounting/stopping in 387.47 seconds at 774.64 MiB. It saves 80.94 seconds/17.3% versus prior v4, 120.61/23.7% versus combined-IQR v2 and 596.81/60.6% versus v1. The deterministic v4 result digest remains unchanged because the compiled features are bit-identical and timing is excluded.

Remaining gap: TVTX is still 87.47 seconds above p95. Review found that M04R-08's written materialization rule says “reconstruct DTW paths only for final results,” but implementation calls Python full-matrix DTW and constructs a path for every one of 9,309 exact candidates. This was not a research omission—the existing frozen POC already shows a compiled two-row distance kernel exactly matching 2,000 references/path lengths at 527.8×, with zero unsafe/mismatch results on 100,000 threshold cases—but it was not wired into completion.

Next correction: Add a distinct v5 contract that uses compiled exact DTW distance without paths during candidate scoring, selects the constrained top-20, then recomputes only those 20 through the original full parent matrix to restore exact tie-order alignments. Require compiled/reference distance equality, final path equality, unchanged ranking/accounting and practical numeric gates. Preserve v1–v4.

L-076 — Deferred-alignment v5 preflight

Implementation: Added a cached, GIL-free, no-fast-math two-row DTW distance kernel to production and explicit m04r-certified-packed-search-v5. v5 requires the v4 requested-position/vector-bound path, scores every exact candidate with normalized DTW distance but no parent matrix, performs the unchanged constrained selection, then rebuilds only the final 20 through the original Python scorer. It fails closed if any recomputed total/component differs by more than 1e−12 before attaching the original exact paths.

Verification: The compiled kernel exactly matches Python and the independent reference on random unequal multivariate shapes, tied constant/step paths and one-dimensional cases while preserving stable predecessor/path-length normalization. On the physical packed fixture, v5 matches v4/brute-force IDs/order, totals/components within 1e−12, and final alignments exactly, with unchanged input/exact accounting and a distinct contract digest. Invalid mode combinations fail closed and the full suite passes.

Next: Run only compiled-structure TVTX v5. Require exact frozen authority paths and unchanged 9,309 accounting/stopping/bound safety. This directly implements the original final-path-only plan; authorize other cases only from measured p95 evidence.

L-077 — Deferred-alignment v5 TVTX PASS; near p95 boundary

Result: TVTX v5 returns exact authority IDs/order/alignments, unchanged 9,309 accounting, strict stopping and safe quantized bounds in 328.03 seconds at 751.00 MiB. Authority total/component deltas remain 8.91e−9/1.12e−7. Certificate/case digests are 7c610635…9050/16808a75…9b3e.

L-078 — Sixteen-worker TVTX control rejected

Controlled result: Holding v5 contract, store, frontier 16,384, block size 2,048 and TVTX fixed, 16 workers complete exactly in 333.97 seconds at 780.57 MiB. Eight workers take 328.03 seconds at 751.00 MiB. Doubling workers is 5.94 seconds/1.8% slower and uses 29.57 MiB more RSS, so eight remains selected.

Why: The pack scan is single-threaded and unchanged; remaining per-group work is already small enough that scheduling/memory-bandwidth overhead offsets extra concurrency. This falsifies the last simple worker-scaling alternative without weakening any gate.

Next: Run historical CAKE and current TAIT sequentially under the selected v5/eight-worker controls. Their timings, together with TVTX 328.03, determine whether the projected 12-case p95 is credible enough to authorize the full checkpointed matrix.

L-079 — v5 heavy-tail check fails p95; compiled quantile kernel prepared

Heavy results: Under selected v5/eight-worker controls, historical CAKE passes in 243.74 seconds with unchanged 7,783 exact rows, but current TAIT takes 355.95 seconds with 7,419. The partial two-case p95 is 350.34 seconds. Together with TVTX at 328.03, the upper tail is not credible for the 300-second full-matrix gate, so the remaining nine cases were not run.

Bound-kernel POC: TAIT is eligible for essentially the complete pack, making query-specific bound math dominant. Added a parallel, GIL-free compiled exact joined-IQR primitive over already-converted float64 samples. The first formula differed by one ULP in 1/4,883 values and was rejected; matching NumPy's endpoint-sensitive interpolation order yields bit-exact quartiles on random, constant and adjacent-float cases.

Measured complete-kernel result: Across fixed 32,768 real TAIT-eligible packed rows, the full quantized bound—not just quartiles—improves from median 1.9649 to 1.1016 seconds, 1.7837×, with totals bit-identical and zero component delta. Focused bound/store/proposal/certified tests and the full suite pass.

Next: Commit this kernel, then rerun TAIT alone under unchanged v5 controls. Require exact candidate/final authority evidence and a material end-to-end reduction before recomputing the heavy-tail projection.

L-084 — Shared exact quartile preparation POC PASS; 24-query gate still open

Diagnosis: The rejected batch shared only physical reads. For every query it separately unpacked and converted candidate arrays, then sorted the same 48 candidate samples together with 48 query samples for every row/channel. Real 32,768-row profiling measured repeated preparation at 11.5–12.0% of three-query bound time.

Rejected controls: Sharing converted arrays alone was exact but achieved 266.29 versus 291.37 seconds at 2,048-row blocks, only 9.42% reduction against the 10% gate. Retesting best-versus-best at the measured 4,096-row optimum yielded 264.18 versus 269.24 seconds, only 1.91%; the apparent gain had largely been a weaker scalar control and was rejected.

Accepted mechanism: Candidate samples are now converted and sorted once per physical block. An exact compiled merge with each sorted query recovers the four order statistics used by NumPy's 25/75 linear percentiles. Five real-row subkernel trials are bit identical and 3.87–4.05× faster for three queries. Scalar packed search uses the same optimized merge but repeats candidate preparation; only batch execution reuses it.

Full proposal result: On the three frozen former-heavy authorities at 4,096 rows, prepared shared scan takes 211.0311 seconds versus 243.8495 for fresh optimized scalar scans: 13.46% less elapsed time, 1.1555× speedup and 543.67 MiB RSS. Every candidate digest, certified-checkpoint digest, eligible count, scalar report and physical-row accounting gate passes. Evidence digest is 2ba6bb05…ad1; the full test suite passes.

Boundary and discovered plan gap: This proves the shared proposal mechanism on three real queries; it is not the frozen 24-query end-to-end gate. Only 12 NASDAQ authorities exist, so the original plan did not define a legitimate 24-query batch registry. Next freeze 24 outcome-blind NASDAQ current/news-style queries and independent scalar controls, then implement grouped exact completion and require unchanged final matches/certificates plus ≥10% complete-pipeline reduction before M04R-08 closes.
